{D4366}]@##/_lhag mthong skyed pa'i thabs bzhugs/_@##/_/dngos 'dzin rnam rtog tshang tshing lta bu shes rab mtshon gyis rab bsal nas/_/de nyid la yang mi gnas de phyir ma rtogs 'gro rnams mun 'thoms la/_/thugs rje chen pos chu klung bzhin du ngang gis 'jug pa'i 
[\[192b.2\]]thabs sna tshogs/_/gsung ba de la dang bas phyag byas lhag mthong skyed thabs mdor bsdu bya/_/sdom ni/_sngon 'gro zhi gnas bsten pa yis/_/rtogs par bya dgos dang pos kyang /_/ngo bo mtshan nyid nges tshig dang /_/dbye ba dmigs pa skyed pa'i thabs/_/de rigs bsod 
[\[192b.3\]]nams yan lag dang /_/lus bcos mtshams sbyor gzhag pa dang /_/nyes spangs ldang sems langs pa yi/_/tshod gzung bskyed rim gnas pa'i tshad/_/bstan pa'i thabs ni ci rigs bya/_/gsal dang thun ring skyed dbyung dang /_/log par rtog pa'i gzhung nyid dang /_/rang 
[\[192b.4\]]phyogs bsgrub med tshul gyis bsgrub/_/de la sngon du 'gro ba ni theg pa chen po'i mdo sde'i tshul gyis rjes 'jug las dang po so sor thar pa'i sdom pa la legs par gnas pa/_smon pa byang chub kyi sems bskyed nas bla ma yon tan dang ldan pa las theg pa chen po'i sdom pa legs 
[\[192b.5\]]par blangs nas gus par slob pa/_tshul khrims rnam par dag pa la sogs pa zhi gnas kyi tshogs la gnas nas/_lhag pa'i sems cung zad brtan pa des lhag mthong gi tshogs la gnas nas lhag pa'i shes rab la bslab pa'i phyir de kho na tshol ba la brtson par bya'o/_/de skad 
[\[192b.6\]]du/_dbu ma'i rgyan las/_gang dag dad pas rjes 'brang ba/_/rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems bskyed nas/_/thub pa'i brtul zhugs blang bya ste/_/de ni yang dag shes tshol brtson/_/zhes gsungs so/_/lhag mthong gi tshogs ni/_'phags pa blo gros mi zad pas bstan pa las/_
[\[192b.7\]]lhag mthong gi tshogs mi zad pa'i skabs nas 'byung ba khong du chud par bya'o/_/de la gang dngos po la mngon par zhen pa de ni nyon mongs pa sna tshogs skye ba'i gzhi yin te/_dper na skyes bu gzugs yid du 'ong ba dang phrad par 'dod cing zhen pas nyon mongs pa skye ba lta bu'o/_/lhag mthong 
[\[193a\]]
[\[193a.1\]]mi skyed par ting nge 'dzin gyi dmigs pa la mngon par zhen pa 'di dag kyang nyon mongs pa yang skye bar 'gyur ro/_/de skad du/_'phags pa ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po las/_ting nge 'dzin de sgom par byed mod kyi/_/de ni bdag tu 'du shes 'jig mi byed/_/de yis nyon 
[\[193a.2\]]mongs phyir zhing rab 'khrugs te/_/lhag spyod 'di na ting 'dzin bsgom pa bzhin/_/zhes gsungs so/_/gang dag nyon mongs pa'i rkyen gyis las bsags pa de dag ni yun ring por 'khor ba na 'khyam zhing sdug bsngal sna tshogs myong ste/_dper na phye ma leb me'i phung por chud pa lta 
[\[193a.3\]]bu'o/_/'on te des tha mal pa'i rtog pa ldog pas grol bar 'gyur zhe na ma yin te/_'phags pa byams pas/_zas chen dug dang 'dres la zhen/_/zhes gsungs so/_/'phags pa klu sgrub kyis kyang /_gang yang rung ba'i gnas rnyed na/_/nyon mongs sbrul gdug 
[\[193a.4\]]g.yon can gyis/_/zin par 'gyur ro gang gi sems/_/gnas med de dag zin mi 'gyur/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de bas na zhen pas zhen pa mi spong ste/_gzhan du na mes tshig pa'i 'phro mer bcug pas sos pa dang gsher ba chur bcug pas bskams pa dang /_ltogs pa zas bcad 
[\[193a.5\]]pas 'drangs pa dang /_skom pa btung ba bcad pas ngoms par thal bar 'gyur ro/_/gzhan dag na re de bzhin gshegs pa la sogs pa gzugs kyi sku'i bdag nyid dam/_gzhan yang rung ste/_shin tu gsal ba mthong na des grol bar 'gyur zhe na/_ma yin te/_'phags pa da ltar gyi 
[\[193a.6\]]sangs rgyas mngon sum du bzhugs pa'i ting nge 'dzin las/_dper na gser gyi gong bu de blang bar bya ba yin yang mes rab tu tsha bar gyur na lag pas gzung bar bya ba ma yin te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gzhi de las lus kyi sdug bsngal skyed pa'i phyir ro/_/de bzhin du gzugs kyi sku de yang blang bar 
[\[193a.7\]]bya ba yin mod kyi 'on kyang zhen par gyur na spang bar bya'o zhes gsungs so/_/sgyu ma lta bur snang ba ni don dam par skye ba med pa ma gtogs par 'byung mi srid par 'phags pa ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po las gsungs so/_/gzhan yang 'di na kha cig de bzhin gshegs pas skye bo blun po 
[\[193b\]]
[\[193b.1\]]shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo'i don ma rtogs shing log par rtogs pa rnams yun ring por 'khor ba na 'khyams pa dag la thabs sna tshogs kyis drang ba'i don du gsungs pa la zhen nas zab mo'i don la mos pa yang zlog par byed de/_'di skad ces 'di ni sgom du mi 'dod rtogs par 
[\[193b.2\]]dka'/_ma rtogs kyang byang chub thob par byed pa'i thabs gzhan yod do/_/gang zhe na/_mtshan ma bsgom pa'o zhes zer zhing gegs byed pa de dag ni lta ba log pa dang gzhan gyis gegs byas pas las kyi sgrib pa shin tu yang spang dka' ba bsags pa'i phyir/_lha dang mi'i lus kyang mi 
[\[193b.3\]]'thob na/_bla na med pa'i byang chub lta ga la 'thob/_de skad du yang /_'phags pa yul 'khor skyong gis zhus pa las/_gang gis chos rnams kyi ni de bzhin nyid/_/ma shes pas na 'gro ba 'khyam 'gyur te/_/thugs rje thabs tshul rig pa brgya dag gis/_/de dag rnams ni ston par 
[\[193b.4\]]mdzad pa lags/_/zhes gsungs so/_/'phags pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya ba las kyang /_gang zag de ni sems can dmyal ba chen por skye bar gsungs la/_gang zag gzhan gyis ni bdud mkhan po la sogs pa'i cha byad du byas pa yin par yang gsungs 
[\[193b.5\]]so/_/de skad du 'phags pa lhas kyang /_gti mug gang gis bsgribs gyur te/_/gang gi yang dag gegs byed pa/_/de la 'bras bu dge ba med/_/thar pa med pa smos ci dgos/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de bas na skyes bu gang bdag dang gzhan gyi 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal dpag tu 
[\[193b.6\]]med pa spong bar 'dod cing /_bla na med pa'i byang chub chen po sku gsum dang ye shes bzhi'i bdag nyid thob par 'dod pas sdug bsngal dpag tu med pa'i rgyu/_nyon mongs pa dug dang 'dra ba'i rtsa ba dngos po la mngon par zhen pa dug gi sdang po lta bu de thabs kyis zin pa'i shes rab 
[\[193b.7\]]mtshon rnon po dang 'dra ba des/_spang ba'i phyir chos rnams kyi de bzhin nyid de ni shin tu rtogs par bya dgos te/_'phags pa dkon mchog sprin las kyang /_rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' dag chos nyid 'di thob nas sems can gzhan dag kyang chos nyid 'di kho na la rab tu dgod do/_/
[\[194a\]]
[\[194a.1\]]snyam ste/_don gang gi phyir 'bad cing rtsol ba dang /_brtson 'grus rtsom pa dang /_dka' ba brgya dag byed pa'i rigs kyi bu de ni de bzhin nyid ces bya'o zhes gsungs so/_/gzhan dag na re chos nyid de rtogs par bya dgos kyang las dang po pas bslab par bya ba ma yin no zhes 
[\[194a.2\]]zer na de lta ma yin te/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa las/_sems bskyed ma thag nas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bslab par bya'o zhes gsungs so/_/'on te de ni thos pa dang bsam pa tsam las bslab par bya ba yin te/_bsgom pa don du gsungs pa ma yin no zhe 
[\[194a.3\]]na/_da ltar mi blta ste/_thos pa dang bsam pa yin mod kyang bsgom pa yang yin te/_der dgag pa'i tshig ni ma gsungs la/_sgrub pa'i tshig ni mang du gsungs so/_/mngon par rtogs pa'i rgyan las kyang tshogs kyi lam gyi skabs su/_rnam par mi rtog ting 'dzin dang /_/zhes gsungs 
[\[194a.4\]]pa dang /_bsgom pa'i rim pa dang po mdzad pa las kyang /_theg pa chen po'i mdo sde yis/_/tshul spyod las ni dang po pa/_/de las brtsams te bsgom pa yi/_/rim pa mdo tsam brjod par bya/_/zhes gsungs la/_de'i lhag mthong bsgom pa'i skabs su/_chos thams cad ngo bo 
[\[194a.5\]]nyid med par dmigs pa med ces gsungs so/_/de lta bas na chos thams cad ngo bo nyid med pa ni las dang po pas kyang shin tu rtogs par bya dgos so/_/de las de rtogs par byed pa ni lhag mthong las gzhan pa ma yin te/_de'i ngo bo nyid ni shes rab bo/_/de skad du/_'phags pa dgongs 
[\[194a.6\]]pa nges par 'grel pa las kyang /_bcom ldan 'das chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid ma mchis pa la/_byang chub sems dpa'i pha rol tu phyin pa gang gis 'dzin/_spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas so/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de'i mtshan nyid ni dbye ba yin te/_de nyid 
[\[194a.7\]]las shes par bya ba'i don de 'byed pa dang rab tu rnam par 'byed par byed pa de ni lhag mthong ngo zhes gsungs so/_/gzhan las kyang shes rab gang zhe na/_de dag nyid la rab tu rnam par 'byed pa'o/_/zhes gsungs so/_/nges pa'i tshig ni 'dir gzhan gyis ma rtogs pa rtogs pa dang ma 
[\[194b\]]
[\[194b.1\]]mthong ba mthong ba ni lhag mthong yin te/_de skad du 'phags pa klu'i rgyal po rgya mtshos zhus pa chen po las/_bcom ldan 'das gal te chos 'ga' zhig mthong na ni/_de ni lhag mthong ma lags so zhes gsungs te/_ma mthong ba de ni mthong ba rnams kyi dam pa yin te/_de skad 
[\[194b.2\]]du/_'phags pa chos kun 'gro ba las kyang /_bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad mi mthong ba ni yang dag par mthong ba'o zhes tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis gsungs so/_/de la dbye ba ni gsum ste/_thos pa'i shes rab dang /_bsam pa'i shes rab dang /_bsgom pa'i shes 
[\[194b.3\]]rab bo/_/de la dmigs pa ni thos pa'i shes rab kyi dmigs pa sgra rang gi mtshan nyid dang /_sgra bkod pa'i yi ge dag la dmigs te/_mngon sum dang dmigs pa mtshungs pa'i phyir ro/_/sgra spyi dang don spyi la yang dmigs te/_mngon sum la nges pa'i shes pas grogs byed 
[\[194b.4\]]pa'i phyir ro/_/de la kha cig na re thos pa'i shes rab ni sgra rang gi mtshan nyid 'ba' zhig la dmigs zer te/_de lta ma yin no/_/gang gi phyir bum pa zhes zer ba'i sgra thos pa'i dus su mgrin pa phra ba la sogs pa'i don ma shes na ma thos pa dang 'dra'o/_/des na bsam pa'i shes 
[\[194b.5\]]rab gang las bskyed/_bsam pa'i shes rab med na bsgom pa'i shes rab mi skye ste/_de'i phyir de ni log par rtog pa yin no/_/bsam pa'i shes rab kyi dmigs pa ni sgra'i spyi dang /_don gyi spyi'o/_/yang gtso bos phye na yang dag pa'i shes pa ni rnam pa gnyis te/_zhes gsungs 
[\[194b.6\]]pas rjes su dpag pa'i shes pa mthar thug pa dang mtshungs par ldan pa'i dmigs pa ni don gyi spyi 'ba' zhig go/_/bsgom pa'i shes rab kyi dmigs pa ni rnam pa gnyis te/_don rang gi mtshan nyid dang /_don gyi spyi'o/_/de la don rang gi mtshan nyid ni 'jig rten las 'das pa'i rnal 
[\[194b.7\]]'byor gyi mngon sum dang mtshungs par ldan pa'i shes rab kyi dmigs pa'o/_/don gyi spyi ni so so'i skye bo'i rjes su dpag pa bsgom pa dang /_mtshungs par ldan pa'i shes rab kyi dmigs pa'o/_/de la skyed pa'i thabs ni gsum ste/_thos pa dang /_bsam pa dang /_bsgom pa'i shes rab bo/_
[\[195a\]]
[\[195a.1\]]de la re zhig thos pa'i shes rab bskyed par bya'o/_/de la kha cig na re thos pa bya mi dgos te/_de ni gzhan la sma bar 'dod pa yin no/_/smra ba la dga' ba ni/_lhag pa'i bsam pa bskul ba'i mdo las/_bdag ni bsgrub nyams da ni ci bya zhes/_/'chi ba'i dus tshe 
[\[195a.2\]]mya ngan chen po ldang /_/gting rnyed ma gyur shin tu sdug bsngal te/_/smra la dga' ba'i nyes pa 'di dag go_/_zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs so/_/de bas na sgom par byed la mdo sde bltas pas chog zer te/_de lta ma yin te/_de ni rnyed pa dang bkur sti'i ched du smra ba la sogs pa 
[\[195a.3\]]gsungs pa yin te/_gzhan la phan pa'i ched du chos smra ba yang phan yon che ba'i phyir dgos pa med pa ma yin no/_/de skad du/_lhag pa'i bsam pa bskul ba'i mdo de nyid las/_byams pa gang thos pa dang /_bkur sti med par chos sbyin pa zang zing med pa'i phan yon nyi shu ste/_gang 
[\[195a.4\]]zhe na/_'di lta ste/_dran pa dang ldan pa yin/_blo dang ldan pa yin/_blo gros dang ldan pa yin/_mos pa dang ldan pa yin/_shes rab dang ldan pa yin/_'jig rten las 'das pa'i shes rab rjes su rtogs pa yin/_'dod chags chung ba yin/_zhe sdang chung ba yin/_gti 
[\[195a.5\]]mug chung ba yin/_bdud kyis de la glags mi rnyed pa yin/_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis de la dgongs pa yin/_mi ma yin pa rnams kyis de la srung ba yin/_lha rnams kyis de'i lus la mdangs 'jug pa yin/_mi mdza' ba rnams kyis de la glags mi rnyed pa 
[\[195a.6\]]yin/_mdza' bo rnams dang mi phyed pa yin/_tshig gzung bar 'os pa yin/_mi 'jigs pa thob pa yin/_yid bde ba mang ba yin/_mkhas pas bsngags pa yin/_de chos kyi sbyin pa rjes su dran par 'gyur ba yin te/_byams pa phan yon nyi shu po 'di dag go zhes gsungs so/_/de 
[\[195a.7\]]ni bstan pa 'dzin par yang gsungs te/_de 'dzin byed pa smra byed dang /_/zhes gsungs te/_de bas na de'i ched du yang thos pa bya dgos so/_/mdo sde bklags pa tsam gyis don rtogs par mi 'gyur te/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i dgongs pa zab mo ni shin tu rtogs par dka'o/_/gsung 
[\[195b\]]
[\[195b.1\]]gcig la yang mos pa sna tshogs 'jug ste/_de skad du/_gsung gcig dka' ni gsal mod kyi/_/du ma nyid du so sor brdas/_/'di bdag kho na'i tshad yin zhes/_/kun gyi de ltar gda' ba ste/_/zhes gsungs pa dang /_lung ni 'dam gyi phur pa 'dra/_/zhes kyang gsungs la/_
[\[195b.2\]]dad ldan rkang brkyang 'dug_/bde gshegs nyi ma 'di/_/bram ze skud 'khal bzhin/_/de nyid dngos las ring /_/zhes gsungs so/_/gzhan yang las dang po pas mdo sde bklags pa tsam gyis don zab mo go na dge ba'i bshes gnyen srog gi phyir yang mi gtang zhes gsungs 
[\[195b.3\]]pa dang yang 'gal la/_klog mkhan kun kyang sde snod gsum par thal bar 'gyur ro/_/de bas na/_sangs rgyas chos ni tshol 'dod na/_/thos pa mang po'i phyir mi 'brangs/_/zhes gsungs pa dge ba'i bshes gnyen brten cing de las thos pa mang po bya ba ni shin tu yang gces so/_/
[\[195b.4\]]de'i phan yon ni sred med kyi bus zhus pa'i mdo las gsungs pa/_rigs kyi bu thos pa dang ldan pa ni shes rab 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/shes rab dang ldan pa ni nyon mongs pa rab tu zhi bar 'gyur ro/_/nyon mongs pa med pa la bdud kyis glags mi rnyed do/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa 
[\[195b.5\]]rgyas par gsungs nas/_byang chub sems dpa' chos 'dod dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid can gyis thos pa'i tshogs la mngon par brtson par bya'o/_/zhes bya ba'i bar du gsungs so/_/de lta bas na phyin ci ma log par ston pa'i dge ba'i bshes gnyen las thos nas de'i don la 
[\[195b.6\]]legs par bsams nas sgom pa la 'jug gi gzhan du nam thos na ci zhig bsam/_ma bsams par ci zhig bsgom ste/_rgyu med pa'i 'bras bu mi srid pa'i phyir ro/_/de bas na slob dpon ka ma la shI las bsgom pa'i rim pa bar du mdzad pa las kyang /_lhag mthong gi tshogs gang zhe na/_
[\[195b.7\]]skyes bu dam pa la brten pa dang /_mang du thos pa yongs su btsal ba dang zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs so/_/yang kha cig na re thos pa kho na bya ste/_tshul khrims ni btang snyoms su gzhag go/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_mi'i lus ni bsnyen gnas tsam gyis kyang 'thob pa'i phyir/_dag 
[\[196a\]]
[\[196a.1\]]slong la sogs pa tshul khrims kyi ched du 'bad pa don med do/_/zhes zer te/_de ltar na 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i yon tan thams cad kyi rten ni tshul khrims yin par mdo sde mtha' dag las gsungs la/_/de'i rtsa ba ni dge slong yin te/_
[\[196a.2\]]de la don med na spang bar bya ba yin pas/_de spangs na rtsa ba chad pas so sor thar pa rigs bdun ka spangs te/_des na bde 'gro thams cad spang ba yin no/_/de spangs pa byang chub bsgrub pa'i rten med pa'i phyir byang chub ji ltar sgrub/_de bas na de skad smra ba des ni mtho ris dang thar pa mtha' 
[\[196a.3\]]dag spang ba yin no/_/'on te bsnyen gnas khas blangs pa ma spangs so/_/zhe na/_dge slong med par de yang mi 'ong la/_mi lus thob pa kho nas kyang mi chog ste/_'di na kha cig 'phen par byed pa'i las kyis mi'i 'gro bar skyes kyang rdzogs par byed pa'i las zhan pas chos la 'jug mi 
[\[196a.4\]]nus pa dag snang ste/_de lta bu'i mi'i lus kyis byang chub ji ltar sgrub/_/de lta bas na bde 'gro khyad par can skyed pa'i phyir tshul khrims khyad par can srog dang 'dra bar bsrung dgos so/_/gzhan du na 'dus byas dag las thar par mi nus shing /_na ba dang rga ba dang 'chi ba dag gis shin tu 
[\[196a.5\]]'joms so/_/de skad du/_'phags pa ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po las kyang /_mi zhig chom rkun mang pos rab gtses nas/_/'tsho bar 'dod pas 'bros shing 'gror btsal na/_/de yi rkang pa 'gro zhing rgyug ma nus/_/chom rkun dag gis de bzung bcom pa ltar/_/de bzhin tshul 
[\[196a.6\]]khrims nyams pa rmongs pa'i mi/_/'dus byas dag las 'bros par 'dod mod kyi/_/tshul khrims nyams pas de yang 'bros mi nus/_/na rga 'chi ba dag gis shin tu 'joms/_/zhes gsungs so/_/'on te shes rab chung ngu thos pa med pa rnams kyi tshul khrims nyams na ngan 'gror 
[\[196a.7\]]skye ste/_shes rab che ba thos pa mang bas tshul khrims nyams kyang ngan 'gror mi 'gro'o zhe na/_de ni bden te mkhas pas ltung ba bshags pa dang bsdams pa byas na ngan 'gror mi ltung ngo /_/de skad du/_lcags kyi ri lu chung yang rgya mtsho'i gting du 'gro/_/de nyid snod du byas na steng na 'phyo ba 
[\[196b\]]
[\[196b.1\]]bzhin/_/de bzhin mi mkhas sdig pa chung ngu byas kyang ngan 'gror 'gro_/mkhas pas sdig pa chen po byas kyang ltung mi 'gyur/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de bas na snod du byas pa dang 'dra bar bshags pa dang bsdams pa byas na mi ltung ba yin gyi thos pa dang shes pa tsam gyis ma yin te/_de 
[\[196b.2\]]skad du/_'phags pa ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po las kyang /_chos kyi rnam grangs ji snyed mang po chub byas kyang /_/thos pas brgyags shing tshul khrims srung bar mi byed na/_/tshul khrims nyams pa des na ngan 'gror 'gro 'gyur te/_/mang du thos pa de la thos pas skyob mi nus/_/zhes 
[\[196b.3\]]gsungs so/_/'o na mang du thos pa dang tshul khrims dag pas lta ba'i ched du rtsod pa la sogs pa nyon mongs pas log par ltung mi nus so zhe na/_de lta ma yin te/_'phags pa ting nge 'dzin mchog dam pa las kyang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis dga' bo la bka' stsal pa/_ngas sngon du dran 
[\[196b.4\]]pa sngon 'das pa'i dus nang mang du thos pa'i skye bor gyur pa'i tshe 'jam dpal dang yod med gnyis la rtsod par gyur te/_'jam dpal ni yod ces smra/_nga ni med do zhes smra ste/_yod med gnyis kyi rtsod pa la bden par gyur te/_gcig tu ma chad do/_/mtha' tshe 'phos nas ngan song gsum 
[\[196b.5\]]du ltung ste/_bskal pa grangs med kyi bar du lcags kyi thu lum za bar gyur to/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de nyid las mang du thos pa'i mi ni ri'am rgya mtsho ltar nga rgyal gyi sems che'o/_/de ltar nga rgyal gyi sems che ba ni ngan song du bskal pa grangs med kyi bar du sdug bsngal myong bar 
[\[196b.6\]]'gyur ro/_/dmyal ba de las thar nas yang 'dab chags kyi byar skye bar 'gyur na mang du thos pa la phan yon ci yod ces gsungs so/_/de lta bas na tshul khrims rnam par dag pa la gnas nas sdug bsngal dang du len pa'i bzod pa dang ldan bas thos pa la bdag dang gzhan gyi don du 'bad par 
[\[196b.7\]]bya zhing nyon mongs pa'i rkyen gyis rtsod par mi bya ste/_de skad du/_'phags pa ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po las/_tshul khrims srung zhing de yis snyems mi bya/_/chos rnams mnyan zhing de yis mi snyems te/_/gang gi shes rab chung ngus de snyems pa/_/rtsa ba de las de yi sdug bsngal 'phel/_
[\[197a\]]
[\[197a.1\]]zhes gsungs so/_/ci zhig thos par bya zhe na/_'dir chos thams cad skye ba med par ston pa'i lung nges pa'i don gyi mdo sde dang de gtan la 'bebs pa'i rigs pa sgron ma lta bu thos par bya'o/_/de la nges pa'i don gyi mdo sde ni gang /_/drang ba'i don gyi mdo sde ni gang 
[\[197a.2\]]zhe na/_'phags pa ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po las kyang /_stong pa bde bar gshegs pas bshad pa ltar/_/nges don mdo sde dag gi bye brag shes/_/gang las sems can gang zag skyes bur bstan/_/chos de thams cad drang ba'i don du shes/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de nyid las nges pa'i don gyi 
[\[197a.3\]]mdo sde'i dbang du byas nas gsungs pa/_nam zhig 'jig rten khams 'di 'byung ba dang /_/'jig rten thams cad nam mkhar 'gyur ba'i tshe/_/ji ltar sngon bzhin phyis kyang de bzhin te/_/chos rnams thams cad de bzhin shes par gyis/_/'jig rten 'di na ji snyed ci yod pa/_/chu yi phung po 'di

[\[202a.1\]]ma mo gsham yin no/_/zhes zer ba lta bu'o/_/bsam pa tsam gyis don mngon sum du mi 'gyur te/_de bas na so sor brtag par bya'o zhes zer ba 'di yang tshig snga phyi 'gal ba yin pas rang bzhin gyi gtan tshigs so/_/de'i phyir de ni gyi na'o/_/gong ma

[\[246a.3\]]de gnyis rab spangs nas/_/mkhas pa dbus la'ang gnas mi byed/_/ces gsungs te/_de ni dbu ma'i mkhan po rnams kyis dbu ma'i bstan bcos kun tu drangs so/_/'phags pa klu sgrub kyis kyang /_rigs pa drug cu pa las/_gang blo yod dang med pa las/_/rnam

[\[260b.1\]
